- Tests: `crates/tungsten-core/src/tests/ui/{paint,anim,prompt,layout,style,route}.rs`, `crates/tungsten-core/src/tests/settings.rs`, `crates/tungsten-render/src/tests/{text_batches,text_nodes}.rs`, `crates/tungsten/src/tests/{state,plugins,ui_route,settings,app,testing_ui}.rs` and example 05's `tests/screens.rs`; smoke's `UI screens` row runs the `menus` fixture.
- Measured at M56's sitting (2026-10-15, `perf-runs/20261015-m56-gameplay-screens/`, five-run suites of every row against 0.75 with an aligned pair): every owned metric of every row not `regressed` in both direct pairs, but `ecs` `bounds_wrap` p50 (0.316 → 0.362 and 0.364 ms), which the aligned pair reads `unchanged` and the symbol's move from 16 to 48 mod 64 explains (`D-083`), and `gpu-throughput` `render_encode` p95 in one pair, a drift metric whose rerun reads `noisy`; first-run digests equal in all eight rows. The 100-widget menu adds 0.020 ms of frame-work p95 at 1080p (budget 0.25 ms); a first open's `render_encode_pre_acquire` excess is +2.690 ms cold and +0.030 ms after a prewarm of 48 runs and 543 glyphs in 2.7–3.2 ms.
- Open limits: a span with its own colour does not fade; interaction-state easing, size tracks and reduced motion are backlog rows; frozen is per root; prewarmed glyphs stay until the atlas needs their room after a full frame's trim, and the atlas's byte size stays unreadable (the T2 row); `SceneEntity` keeps its shared-`StateId` limit.

## D-164 — Rebinding (M56): the router's capture-next-input mode, and binding labels and prompts resolved after each routing against the action map and the last device
**Date:** 2026-10-15
**Decision:** `UiRouter::capture_next_input(widget)` starts a capture from the next routing; `cancel_capture()` ends it and `capturing()` names it. While it is pending every key, mouse button, pad button and stick direction press is the UI's (`wants_keyboard` holds) and none navigates or repeats; the first that is not an OS repeat ends it with `UiEvent::InputCaptured { widget, binding }` (`EventQueue::<UiEvent>::input_captured`), its release staying the UI's and a stick's axis withheld until it. Escape is captured like any key (Q9); the wheel is taken, neither captured nor routed (Q10); a key with no `input.json` name is reported and `D-159`'s persist skips it. Window focus loss, `cancel_capture`, a new capture, or the widget removed, hidden, disabled or frozen ends it with `UiEvent::CaptureCancelled`. `Binding::label` names a binding for a prompt: the physical key's US label (`W`, `Space`, `Esc`, `Left Shift`, `Page Up`, `Key <n>`), `Left Mouse`, `Wheel Up`, Xbox-style pad buttons (`A` for South, `LB`, `D-Pad Up`) and stick directions (`Left Stick Up`), with tables beside the `input.json` names. `UiTree::set_prompt(id, template)` gives a label or a button's label a template whose `{action:NAME}` placeholders show, for the last device, the first binding of its kind (keys and pointer: key, mouse, wheel; pad: button, stick), else the first of any, else `Unbound`; every other brace is literal and `set_text` clears it. `ui_route_system` resolves prompts after each routing, rebuilding one only when the device kind or an action's bindings differ from its last resolution, so a reload's whole-map replacement and `replace_bindings` both show, and an unchanged prompt costs slice compares.

**Why:** M4's settings screen needs one rebind and its screens need prompts that follow the pad. Capturing in the router, not in a game system reading raw input, keeps the press and its release from the routed view and from `engine_exit`, so neither gameplay nor the exit check sees half a press. Comparing bindings rather than keeping a revision survives the reload, which replaces the `ActionMap` resource whole (A13). Xbox letters follow SDL's reference layout and the common PC pad; physical US key labels are what winit 0.30 offers without a keymap query.

**Consequences:**
- Amends `D-149` as its marker line says; extends `D-045` (labels beside the names) and `D-040` (`InputCaptured`, `CaptureCancelled`).
- Tests: `crates/tungsten-core/src/tests/input/key_serde.rs`, `crates/tungsten-core/src/tests/ui/{prompt,route}.rs` and the harness tests in `crates/tungsten/src/tests/ui_route.rs`; example 05's settings screen rebinds `jump`, replacing only the captured kind's bindings and persisting through the user layer.
- Open limits: an AZERTY player sees US labels and a Nintendo pad swapped ones; prompt icons and keymap-aware names are backlog rows; the owner's gamepad playthrough waits for the backend's re-land.
